`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/README.md`:

```md
# VyxAC - low-false-positive anticheat for Spigot / Paper / Purpur (1.20.x - 1.21.x)

## Build and install
1. Needs JDK 17+ and Maven. In this folder run: `mvn package`
2. Copy `target/VyxAC-1.0.0.jar` into your server's `plugins/` folder, start the server once.
3. Edit `plugins/VyxAC/config.yml` (every check gets its own section on first start) or use `/vyxac`.

Optional pom change: set `spigot-api` to the version you run (1.21.x works too, Java 21 on the server is fine).

## How it avoids false bans
* Default mode is **REVIEW**: nobody is punished automatically, staff get `[Punish] [Dismiss] [TP]` buttons.
* In AUTO mode a punishment needs **several different checks** (default 2) that each reached their own punish-VL,
  at least 6 flags inside 3 minutes, the player's ping <= 400, a stable server (TPS guard), no join/teleport grace,
  and the evidence must still hold after a short delay. The mini AI counts as one extra piece of evidence only.
* Punishment ladder (kick -> kick -> ban) instead of instant bans; a punishment resets the player's VL.
* Checks use accumulated evidence (debt / buffers), never single events, and are suspended during: join, teleport,
  respawn, world change, knockback/explosions, vehicles, flight, elytra/riptide, pistons, creative/spectator,
  server lag (TPS / long ticks) and connection stalls.
* Unknown world data (unloaded chunk) is always treated as "player is legitimately supported".
* Everything is configurable per check: `enabled`, `punishable`, `vl-per-flag`, `alert-vl`, `punish-vl`, `mitigate-vl`, `params.*`.

## Mini AI (low CPU)
A small statistical model, not a neural network: O(1) updates per hit, analysis every 4 s, a hard time budget
(`ai.max-micros-per-second`, default 3 ms/s) with round-robin so it can never lag the server. It scores click rhythm,
aim precision, and reaction constancy, and compares players with the server's own population of *clean* players.
It raises its own alerts and corroborates other checks; it never punishes alone. `/vyxac ai` shows its cost.

## Commands (permission `vyxac.admin`, alerts need `vyxac.alerts`, `vyxac.bypass` exempts a player)
| Command | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `/vyxac help` | list commands |
| `/vyxac reload` | reload config.yml |
| `/vyxac configure [path] [value]` | read/change ANY config value (TAB completes paths) |
| `/vyxac customize` | GUI: enable/disable checks, evidence on/off, mode, AI, mitigation, lag guard, alerts |
| `/vyxac special [feature] [value]` | mode, mitigation, lag-protection, ai, ai-sensitivity, logging, require-swing, bedrock-exempt, console-alerts, webhook, flood-kick |
| `/vyxac check <name> [on/off]` | toggle one check |
| `/vyxac alerts [on/off]` | your alerts |
| `/vyxac status` / `ai` | health, TPS guard, AI cost |
| `/vyxac profile/history <player>` | violations, AI confidence, last flags |
| `/vyxac clear <player/all>`, `exempt <player> [sec]` | reset / temporarily ignore |
| `/vyxac punish/dismiss <player>` | used by the review buttons |

## The 47 checks
The list you gave contains FastBow twice (#10 and #45); it is one check here.
Combat: KillAura, AimAssist, AutoClicker, TriggerBot, Reach, Hitbox, Criticals, Velocity, AntiKnockback, FastBow, AutoPot, AutoTotem, MultiAura, AutoCrystal, Backtrack.
Movement: Speed, Fly, LongJump, BunnyHop, NoFall, Jesus, Spider, Step, NoSlow, Timer, Blink, Phase, FastLadder, ElytraFly.
Player: AutoArmor, ChestStealer, InventoryMove, Freecam, XRay, Scaffold, FastPlace, Nuker, FastBreak, Baritone, AutoFish.
Packet/misc: PingSpoof, BadPackets, PacketFlood, GroundSpoof, RotationSpoof, FastEat, AutoEat.
Plus the pseudo-check `AI`.

## Honest limitations - read before going live
* **Not run on a live server.** It was syntax/flow-checked and its core math was unit-tested and simulated,
  but it could not be compiled against the real Spigot jar in the build sandbox (offline). Compile it yourself;
  if the compiler reports an API mismatch for your version, that is a quick fix.
* **Start in REVIEW or ALERT_ONLY** for a few days, look at `/vyxac profile` and the logs in `plugins/VyxAC/logs/`,
  then tune thresholds. No anticheat can promise zero false positives.
* **Bukkit-only, no packet library.** Timer, Blink, PacketFlood, PingSpoof and BadPackets are approximations built
  from movement events. Blink and PingSpoof are alert-only by default. For exact versions add PacketEvents/ProtocolLib.
* Speed hacks below roughly 1.2x and similar subtle advantages are tolerated on purpose (no false bans first).
* Geometry combat checks (Reach/Hitbox/Backtrack/KillAura angle) run on player-vs-player hits only.
* X-Ray is a heuristic (alert only). Real protection: enable the server's built-in anti-xray (Paper: `anti-xray` in
  `paper-world-defaults.yml`; Spigot: `anti-xray` in `spigot.yml`).
* Plugins that break many blocks or deal damage via API (VeinMiner, skill plugins) can look like Nuker / MultiAura:
  give those players `vyxac.bypass` or disable that check. `general.require-swing` already ignores most API damage.
* Bedrock players (Floodgate prefix, default `.`) are exempt.

```

```xml
        <maven.compiler.target>17</maven.compiler.target>
        <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>

            <groupId>org.spigotmc</groupId>
            <artifactId>spigot-api</artifactId>

        <plugins>

                <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>
                <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>
                <version>3.11.0</version>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </build>
</project>
```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/AiMonitor.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * The "mini AI": a very small statistical model, not a neural network, so it costs almost no CPU.
 *
 *  - Combat hooks only do O(1) updates (a few additions per hit).
 *  - Every few seconds each player with enough hits is scored from three features
 *    (click rhythm, aim precision, reaction constancy) plus how far the player is from the server's own
 *    population of clean players (the baseline learns from players that have no violations).
 *  - A hard time budget per second guarantees the analysis can never lag the server; players that do not fit
 *    in the budget are simply analysed on the next run (round robin).
 *
 * The AI never punishes alone: it raises its own alerts and acts as one extra piece of evidence for PunishManager.
 */
public final class AiMonitor {
    /** Online mean / variance (Welford) with a cap so the baseline keeps adapting. */
    private static final class Welford {
        int n;
        double mean, m2;

        void add(double x) {
            if (n >= 4000) { // forget slowly
                m2 *= (n - 1) / (double) n;
                n--;
            }
            n++;
            double d = x - mean;
            mean += d / n;
            m2 += d * (x - mean);
        }

        double std() {
            return n > 1 ? Math.sqrt(m2 / (n - 1)) : 0;
        }
    }

    private final VyxAC pl;
    private BukkitTask task;
    private int intervalSec = 4, minAttacks = 20;
    private double sensitivity = 1.0, alertConf = 0.88;
    private long budgetMicrosPerSec = 3000;

    private final Welford popAimStd = new Welford(), popMode = new Welford();
    private int rr;
    private long runs, skipped, totalNanos, lastRunNanos, analysed;

    public AiMonitor(VyxAC pl) {
        this.pl = pl;
    }

    public void reload(FileConfiguration c) {
        intervalSec = Math.max(1, c.getInt("ai.interval-seconds", 4));
        minAttacks = Math.max(8, c.getInt("ai.min-attacks", 20));
        sensitivity = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(3.0, c.getDouble("ai.sensitivity", 1.0)));
        alertConf = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(0.999, c.getDouble("ai.alert-confidence", 0.88)));
        budgetMicrosPerSec = Math.max(200, c.getLong("ai.max-micros-per-second", 3000));
        stop();
        if (pl.settings().aiEnabled) {
            long period = intervalSec * 20L;
            task = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(pl, this::run, period, period);
        }
    }

    public void stop() {
        if (task != null) {
            task.cancel();
            task = null;
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ O(1) hooks (called from the combat listener)

    /** @param reactionMs time from the last big camera move to this hit, or -1 when unknown */
    public void onHit(PlayerData pd, double aimErrDeg, long now, double reactionMs) {
        if (!pl.settings().aiEnabled) return;
        AiStats st = pd.ai;
        if (st.lastHitMs != 0) {
            long gap = now - st.lastHitMs;
            if (gap < 1500) st.addInterval((int) Math.round(gap / 50.0));
        }
        st.lastHitMs = now;
        st.hits++;
        if (aimErrDeg >= 0) st.addAim(aimErrDeg);
        if (reactionMs >= 0 && reactionMs < 600) st.addReaction(reactionMs);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ periodic analysis

    private void run() {
        if (!pl.settings().aiEnabled) return;
        long t0 = System.nanoTime();
        long budget = budgetMicrosPerSec * 1000L * intervalSec;
        List<PlayerData> list = new ArrayList<>(pl.allData());
        int n = list.size();
        if (n == 0) return;
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        for (int k = 0; k < n; k++) {
            if (System.nanoTime() - t0 > budget) {
                rr = (rr + k) % n;
                skipped++;
                break;
            }
            analyse(list.get((rr + k) % n), now);
        }
        lastRunNanos = System.nanoTime() - t0;
        totalNanos += lastRunNanos;
        runs++;
    }

    private static double clamp01(double v) {
        return v < 0 ? 0 : (v > 1 ? 1 : v);
    }

    private void analyse(PlayerData pd, long now) {
        AiStats st = pd.ai;
        if (st.hits == 0) return;
        if (now - st.lastHitMs > 90_000L) { // stale window: start over
            st.resetWindow();
            st.hotWindows = 0;
            return;
        }
        if (st.hits < minAttacks) return;
        if (pd.bypassed(now, pl.settings())) {
            st.resetWindow();
            return;
        }
        analysed++;

        double mode = st.modeRatio();
        double aimMean = st.aimMean, aimStd = st.aimStd();
        double reStd = st.reStd();

        // feature 1: click rhythm too regular (only meaningful at a real click speed)
        double s1 = mode <= 0 ? 0 : clamp01((mode - 0.55) / 0.40);
        // feature 2: aim too precise AND too consistent
        double s2 = st.aimN >= 10 ? clamp01((3.0 - aimMean) / 3.0) * clamp01((2.5 - aimStd) / 2.5) : 0;
        // feature 3: the delay between camera move and hit is constant and very short
        double s3 = st.reN >= 8 ? clamp01((18.0 - reStd) / 18.0) * (st.reMean < 120 ? 1.0 : 0.3) : 0;
        // feature 4: how unusual is this aim consistency for THIS server's players
        double pop = 0;
        if (popAimStd.n >= 40 && st.aimN >= 10) {
            double z = (popAimStd.mean - aimStd) / Math.max(popAimStd.std(), 0.25);
            pop = clamp01(z / 4.0);
        }

        double x = 0.9 * s1 + 1.2 * s2 + 0.9 * s3 + 0.4 * pop;
        double raw = 1.0 / (1.0 + Math.exp(-5.0 * sensitivity * (x - 1.0)));
        boolean fresh = now - pd.aiConfAt < 60_000L;
        double conf = fresh ? 0.5 * pd.aiConfidence + 0.5 * raw : raw;
        pd.aiConfidence = conf;
        pd.aiConfAt = now;
        st.lastScore = raw;

        // learn the population from clean players only
        boolean clean = pd.totalVl() < 0.5 && now - pd.lastFlagMs > 120_000L && conf < 0.5;
        if (clean) {
            if (st.aimN >= 10) popAimStd.add(aimStd);
            popMode.add(mode);
        }

        if (conf >= alertConf) st.hotWindows++;
        else st.hotWindows = Math.max(0, st.hotWindows - 1);
        if (st.hotWindows >= 2) {
            String info = String.format("conf %.0f%% (rhythm %.2f, aim %.2f, reaction %.2f, pop %.2f)",
                    conf * 100, s1, s2, s3, pop);
            pl.vio().flag(pd, pl.checks().get("AI"), info, 1.0);
        }
        st.resetWindow();
    }

    public void resetPlayer(PlayerData pd) {
        pd.ai.resetWindow();
        pd.ai.hotWindows = 0;
        pd.aiConfidence = 0;
    }

    public String status() {
        double avgMicros = runs == 0 ? 0 : totalNanos / 1000.0 / runs;
        return String.format("runs=%d, avg=%.0fus/run, last=%.0fus, budget=%dus/s, analysed=%d, skipped-runs=%d, baseline-samples=%d",
                runs, avgMicros, lastRunNanos / 1000.0, budgetMicrosPerSec, analysed, skipped, popAimStd.n);
    }
}

```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/AiStats.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

/** Rolling per-player combat statistics used by the mini AI. O(1) updates, a few hundred bytes per player. */
public final class AiStats {
    public final int[] iv = new int[32];
    public int ivN, ivI;
    public long lastHitMs;
    public int aimN, reN, hits, hotWindows;
    public double aimMean, aimM2, reMean, reM2, lastScore;

    public void addInterval(int ticks) {
        iv[ivI] = ticks;
        ivI = (ivI + 1) % iv.length;
        if (ivN < iv.length) ivN++;
    }

    public void addAim(double deg) {
        aimN++;
        double d = deg - aimMean;
        aimMean += d / aimN;
        aimM2 += d * (deg - aimMean);
    }

    public void addReaction(double ms) {
        reN++;
        double d = ms - reMean;
        reMean += d / reN;
        reM2 += d * (ms - reMean);
    }

    public double aimStd() { return aimN > 1 ? Math.sqrt(aimM2 / (aimN - 1)) : 0; }
    public double reStd() { return reN > 1 ? Math.sqrt(reM2 / (reN - 1)) : 0; }

    /** Share of recent click intervals that equal the most common interval (in ticks). */
    public double modeRatio() {
        if (ivN < 12) return 0;
        int[] counts = new int[64];
        int best = 0;
        for (int k = 0; k < ivN; k++) {
            int v = Math.min(63, Math.max(0, iv[k]));
            if (++counts[v] > best) best = counts[v];
        }
        return best / (double) ivN;
    }

    public void resetWindow() {
        aimN = 0;
        aimMean = aimM2 = 0;
        reN = 0;
        reMean = reM2 = 0;
        hits = 0;
    }
}

```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/CheckInfo.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/** Definition + live settings of one check. The same object is mutated on reload, so references stay valid. */
public final class CheckInfo {
    public final String name, category, description;
    public boolean enabled = true, punishable;
    /** VL added per flag (multiplied by the flag weight) */
    public double vlPerFlag;
    /** alerts are only sent once the VL reaches this value */
    public double alertVl;
    /** the check only counts as punishment evidence once its VL reaches this value */
    public double punishVl;
    /** hits / moves are only cancelled (mitigated) once the VL reaches this value */
    public double mitigateVl;
    final Map<String, Double> defaults = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    final Map<String, Double> params = new HashMap<>();

    CheckInfo(String name, String category, String description) {
        this.name = name;
        this.category = category;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public double p(String key) {
        Double v = params.get(key);
        if (v == null) v = defaults.get(key);
        return v == null ? 0.0 : v;
    }
}

```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/CheckRegistry.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

import org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * All checks and their default settings. Every value can be changed in config.yml under
 * checks.&lt;Name&gt;.* or with /vyxac configure and /vyxac customize.
 */
public final class CheckRegistry {
    private final Map<String, CheckInfo> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    public CheckRegistry() {
        defineAll();
    }

    private void def(String name, String cat, String desc, boolean punishable,
                     double vlPerFlag, double alertVl, double punishVl, double mitigateVl, Object... kv) {
        CheckInfo c = new CheckInfo(name, cat, desc);
        c.punishable = punishable;
        c.vlPerFlag = vlPerFlag;
        c.alertVl = alertVl;
        c.punishVl = punishVl;
        c.mitigateVl = mitigateVl;
        for (int i = 0; i + 1 < kv.length; i += 2) {
            c.defaults.put((String) kv[i], ((Number) kv[i + 1]).doubleValue());
        }
        map.put(name, c);
    }

    private void defineAll() {
        final double NO = 9999; // "never mitigate"

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ combat
        def("KillAura", "combat", "Hits that no human could land (angle, instant target switching)", true, 1.0, 1, 8, 3,
                "max-angle", 95, "switch-angle", 60, "switch-ms", 250, "min-distance", 1.0, "buffer", 4);
        def("AimAssist", "combat", "Unnaturally precise aim lock on moving targets", true, 1.0, 2, 10, NO,
                "lock-error", 1.2, "window", 12, "min-lock", 10, "min-distance", 1.5);
        def("AutoClicker", "combat", "Inhuman click speed or click rhythm", true, 1.0, 2, 10, 5,
                "max-cps", 20, "mode-ratio", 0.9, "min-cps", 7);
        def("TriggerBot", "combat", "Attacks with a machine-constant delay after the crosshair moves", true, 1.0, 2, 10, NO,
                "max-reaction-ms", 70, "max-std-ms", 12, "samples", 10);
        def("Reach", "combat", "Hits from further away than the game allows", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "tolerance", 0.45);
        def("Hitbox", "combat", "Hits while the crosshair is not on the target", true, 1.0, 1, 8, 3,
                "expand", 0.3);
        def("Criticals", "combat", "Critical hits without really falling", true, 1.0, 2, 8, 4,
                "max-fall", 0.5, "buffer", 4);
        def("Velocity", "combat", "Takes much less knockback than expected", true, 1.0, 2, 8, NO,
                "min-ratio", 0.3, "buffer", 3, "min-kb", 0.25);
        def("AntiKnockback", "combat", "Takes no knockback at all", true, 1.0, 2, 8, NO,
                "min-ratio", 0.1, "buffer", 3);
        def("FastBow", "combat", "Fires a bow faster than the charge time allows", true, 1.0, 1, 5, 1,
                "tolerance-ms", 150, "force-slack", 0.06);
        def("AutoPot", "combat", "Throws potions within a tick of switching slots", true, 1.0, 2, 8, NO,
                "min-delay-ms", 70, "buffer", 3);
        def("AutoTotem", "combat", "Re-equips a totem faster than a human can react", true, 1.0, 2, 8, NO,
                "max-reaction-ms", 90, "buffer", 3);
        def("MultiAura", "combat", "Attacks several targets in the same tick", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "same-tick-ms", 35);
        def("AutoCrystal", "combat", "Places and detonates end crystals with machine timing", true, 1.0, 2, 8, NO,
                "max-reaction-ms", 60, "buffer", 4);
        def("Backtrack", "combat", "Hits targets at positions older than lag compensation allows", true, 0.7, 1, 8, 3,
                "extra-ms", 400);

        // ---------------------------------------------------------- movement
        def("Speed", "movement", "Moves faster than vanilla physics allow", true, 1.0, 1, 8, 3,
                "tolerance", 1.0, "leniency", 1.12, "ice-multiplier", 2.2);
        def("Fly", "movement", "Hovers or rises without support", true, 1.0, 1, 8, 3,
                "tolerance", 0.55, "hover-limit", 8);
        def("LongJump", "movement", "Jumps further than the airtime allows", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "leniency", 1.35, "slack", 0.6);
        def("BunnyHop", "movement", "Chains hops faster than vanilla momentum allows", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 3,
                "leniency", 1.28, "runs", 4);
        def("NoFall", "movement", "Lands from a height without taking fall damage", true, 1.0, 1, 5, 2,
                "min-drop", 4.0, "wait-ticks", 4);
        def("Jesus", "movement", "Walks on liquids", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 3,
                "buffer", 8);
        def("Spider", "movement", "Climbs walls without a ladder", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 3,
                "tolerance", 0.55);
        def("Step", "movement", "Steps up blocks higher than allowed", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "tolerance", 0.12);
        def("NoSlow", "movement", "Moves at normal speed while using an item", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 3,
                "max-speed", 0.12, "buffer", 6, "min-use-ms", 400);
        def("Timer", "movement", "Runs the game clock faster than normal", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 4,
                "tolerance-ms", 450, "bank-ms", 4000);
        def("Blink", "movement", "Holds movement back and releases it in a burst (alert only: lagging clients look similar)", false, 1.0, 1, 6, NO,
                "min-gap-ms", 600, "burst", 8, "stable-ping-delta", 80, "buffer", 3);
        def("Phase", "movement", "Moves through solid blocks", true, 1.0, 1, 5, 1,
                "buffer", 2);
        def("FastLadder", "movement", "Climbs ladders and vines too fast", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 3,
                "max-up", 0.27, "buffer", 4);
        def("ElytraFly", "movement", "Impossible elytra speed or hovering", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 3,
                "max-speed", 3.2, "hover-limit", 8);

        // ------------------------------------------------- player / inventory
        def("AutoArmor", "player", "Equips armor in consecutive ticks", true, 1.0, 2, 8, NO,
                "max-interval-ms", 55, "buffer", 3);
        def("ChestStealer", "player", "Empties containers faster than a human can click", true, 1.0, 2, 8, NO,
                "max-interval-ms", 55, "buffer", 8);
        def("InventoryMove", "player", "Keeps moving while a container is open", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 3,
                "min-open-ms", 600, "min-speed", 0.15, "buffer", 6);
        def("Freecam", "player", "Interacts with a block face the player cannot see", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 1,
                "tolerance", 0.5);
        def("XRay", "player", "Mines hidden ores far more often than chance allows (needs staff review)", false, 1.0, 1, 5, NO,
                "min-hidden", 5, "min-ratio", 0.08, "window-sec", 600);
        def("Scaffold", "player", "Places blocks without looking at them", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "expand", 0.25, "buffer", 4);
        def("FastPlace", "player", "Places blocks faster than allowed", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "max-per-sec", 14);
        def("Nuker", "player", "Breaks many blocks at once", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "max-per-sec", 16, "same-tick", 4);
        def("FastBreak", "player", "Breaks blocks faster than tool speed allows", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "tolerance", 0.6);
        def("Baritone", "player", "Bot-like movement: sprinting sideways relative to the camera", false, 1.0, 1, 6, NO,
                "min-offset-deg", 75, "buffer", 12);
        def("AutoFish", "player", "Reels in a fish with machine timing", false, 1.0, 1, 6, NO,
                "max-reaction-ms", 110, "buffer", 4);

        // --------------------------------------------------- packet / misc
        def("PingSpoof", "packet", "Reported latency swings that real networks do not produce (approximate)", false, 1.0, 1, 6, NO,
                "delta", 300, "buffer", 6);
        def("BadPackets", "packet", "Invalid movement or rotation values", true, 1.0, 1, 4, 1);
        def("PacketFlood", "packet", "Sends far more packets than a client does", true, 1.0, 1, 5, NO,
                "max-moves", 60, "max-clicks", 60, "kick-at", 0);
        def("GroundSpoof", "packet", "Claims to be on the ground while in the air", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 3,
                "buffer", 5);
        def("RotationSpoof", "packet", "Impossible or machine-constant rotations", true, 1.0, 1, 6, 2,
                "const-steps", 8);
        def("FastEat", "packet", "Finishes eating or drinking too quickly", true, 1.0, 1, 5, 1,
                "tolerance", 0.7);
        def("AutoEat", "packet", "Switches slot and eats with machine timing", false, 1.0, 2, 8, NO,
                "min-delay-ms", 70, "buffer", 4);

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ mini AI
        def("AI", "ai", "Statistical anomaly model (only ever corroborates, never punishes alone)", false, 1.0, 1, 9999, NO);
    }

    public CheckInfo get(String name) {
        CheckInfo c = map.get(name);
        if (c == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Unknown check: " + name);
        return c;
    }

    public CheckInfo find(String name) {
        for (CheckInfo c : map.values()) if (c.name.equalsIgnoreCase(name)) return c;
        return null;
    }

    public Collection<CheckInfo> all() {
        return map.values();
    }

    /** Adds every check's defaults so a fresh config.yml gets complete sections. */
    public void registerDefaults(FileConfiguration c) {
        for (CheckInfo ci : map.values()) {
            String b = "checks." + ci.name + ".";
            c.addDefault(b + "enabled", true);
            c.addDefault(b + "punishable", ci.punishable);
            c.addDefault(b + "vl-per-flag", ci.vlPerFlag);
            c.addDefault(b + "alert-vl", ci.alertVl);
            c.addDefault(b + "punish-vl", ci.punishVl);
            c.addDefault(b + "mitigate-vl", ci.mitigateVl);
            for (Map.Entry<String, Double> e : ci.defaults.entrySet()) {
                c.addDefault(b + "params." + e.getKey(), e.getValue());
            }
        }
    }

    public void load(FileConfiguration c) {
        for (CheckInfo ci : map.values()) {
            String b = "checks." + ci.name + ".";
            ci.enabled = c.getBoolean(b + "enabled", true);
            ci.punishable = c.getBoolean(b + "punishable", ci.punishable);
            ci.vlPerFlag = c.getDouble(b + "vl-per-flag", ci.vlPerFlag);
            ci.alertVl = c.getDouble(b + "alert-vl", ci.alertVl);
            ci.punishVl = c.getDouble(b + "punish-vl", ci.punishVl);
            ci.mitigateVl = c.getDouble(b + "mitigate-vl", ci.mitigateVl);
            for (Map.Entry<String, Double> e : ci.defaults.entrySet()) {
                ci.params.put(e.getKey(), c.getDouble(b + "params." + e.getKey(), e.getValue()));
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/CombatListener.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.EnderCrystal;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.entity.ThrownPotion;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityResurrectEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityShootBowEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.ProjectileLaunchEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerAnimationEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerAnimationType;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerSwapHandItemsEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;

/**
 * Combat checks. Geometry checks (Reach, Hitbox, Backtrack, KillAura angle) only run on player-vs-player hits,
 * use the attacker's ping to look back at the target's recent positions (lag compensation) and use wide tolerances.
 */
public final class CombatListener implements Listener {
    private final VyxAC pl;
    private final CheckInfo KA, AIM, AC, TRIG, REACH, HITBOX, CRIT, FASTBOW, AUTOPOT, TOTEM, MULTI, CRYSTAL, BACK;

    public CombatListener(VyxAC pl) {
        this.pl = pl;
        CheckRegistry r = pl.checks();
        KA = r.get("KillAura");
        AIM = r.get("AimAssist");
        AC = r.get("AutoClicker");
        TRIG = r.get("TriggerBot");
        REACH = r.get("Reach");
        HITBOX = r.get("Hitbox");
        CRIT = r.get("Criticals");
        FASTBOW = r.get("FastBow");
        AUTOPOT = r.get("AutoPot");
        TOTEM = r.get("AutoTotem");
        MULTI = r.get("MultiAura");
        CRYSTAL = r.get("AutoCrystal");
        BACK = r.get("Backtrack");
    }

    // ===================================================================================== helpers

    /** Smallest distance from the eye to the target's hitbox over the lag-compensation window. */
    private static double minDist(PlayerData t, Player tp, double ex, double ey, double ez, long now, long windowMs) {
        double hw = 0.3 * t.scale, hh = 1.8 * t.scale;
        Location cur = tp.getLocation();
        double best = Geo.pointBoxDist(ex, ey, ez, cur.getX() - hw, cur.getY(), cur.getZ() - hw, cur.getX() + hw, cur.getY() + hh, cur.getZ() + hw);
        for (int i = 0; i < t.hCount; i++) {
            int k = t.histIdx(i);
            boolean past = now - t.hT[k] > windowMs; // the first sample older than the window was still valid at its start
            double d = Geo.pointBoxDist(ex, ey, ez, t.hX[k] - hw, t.hY[k], t.hZ[k] - hw, t.hX[k] + hw, t.hY[k] + hh, t.hZ[k] + hw);
            if (d < best) best = d;
            if (past) break;
        }
        return best;
    }

    private static boolean rayHit(double ox, double oy, double oz, double dx, double dy, double dz, double maxT,
                                  PlayerData t, Player tp, long now, long windowMs, double expand) {
        double hw = 0.3 * t.scale + expand, hh = 1.8 * t.scale + expand;
        Location cur = tp.getLocation();
        if (Geo.rayHitsBox(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, maxT, cur.getX() - hw, cur.getY() - expand, cur.getZ() - hw,
                cur.getX() + hw, cur.getY() + hh, cur.getZ() + hw)) {
            return true;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < t.hCount; i++) {
            int k = t.histIdx(i);
            boolean past = now - t.hT[k] > windowMs;
            if (Geo.rayHitsBox(ox, oy, oz, dx, dy, dz, maxT, t.hX[k] - hw, t.hY[k] - expand, t.hZ[k] - hw,
                    t.hX[k] + hw, t.hY[k] + hh, t.hZ[k] + hw)) {
                return true;
            }
            if (past) break;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ===================================================================================== swings

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onSwing(PlayerAnimationEvent e) {
        if (e.getAnimationType() != PlayerAnimationType.ARM_SWING) return;
        PlayerData pd = pl.data(e.getPlayer());
        pd.lastSwingMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
        pd.evSwings++;
    }

    // ===================================================================================== hits

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onHit(EntityDamageByEntityEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getDamager() instanceof Player p)) return;
        if (e.getCause() != EntityDamageEvent.DamageCause.ENTITY_ATTACK) return;
        Settings s = pl.settings();
        if (!s.enabled) return;
        PlayerData pd = pl.data(p);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (pd.bypassed(now, s)) return;
        Entity target = e.getEntity();
        long prevAttack = pd.lastAttackMs;
        pd.lastAttackMs = now;

        try {
            if (target instanceof EnderCrystal) {
                if (crystal(pd, target, now)) e.setCancelled(true);
                return;
            }
            if (s.requireSwing && now - pd.lastSwingMs > 1200) return; // damage that did not come from a real click (plugins)
            GameMode gm = p.getGameMode();
            if (gm == GameMode.CREATIVE || gm == GameMode.SPECTATOR) return;
            if (s.lagProtection && pl.ticks().unstable()) return;
            if (pd.ping > s.maxPing || now - pd.lastTeleport < 1000 || now - pd.lastRespawn < 2000 || pd.exotic) return;
            if (analyse(e, p, pd, target, now, prevAttack)) e.setCancelled(true);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            if (now - pd.ts("err") > 60_000L) {
                pd.ts("err", now);
                pl.getLogger().warning("Combat check error: " + t);
            }
        }
    }

    /** returns true when the hit should be cancelled (mitigation) */
    private boolean analyse(EntityDamageByEntityEvent e, Player p, PlayerData pd, Entity target, long now, long prevAttack) {
        Settings s = pl.settings();
        boolean cancel = false;
        Player tp = target instanceof Player t ? t : null;
        PlayerData pd2 = tp != null ? pl.dataOrNull(tp.getUniqueId()) : null;

        Location eye = p.getEyeLocation();
        double ex = eye.getX(), ey = eye.getY(), ez = eye.getZ();
        float yaw = eye.getYaw(), pitch = eye.getPitch();
        double ldx = Geo.dirX(yaw, pitch), ldy = Geo.dirY(pitch), ldz = Geo.dirZ(yaw, pitch);
        BoundingBox tb = target.getBoundingBox();
        double aimErr = Geo.angleDeg(ldx, ldy, ldz, tb.getCenterX() - ex, tb.getCenterY() - ey, tb.getCenterZ() - ez);
        double dist = Geo.pointBoxDist(ex, ey, ez, tb.getMinX(), tb.getMinY(), tb.getMinZ(), tb.getMaxX(), tb.getMaxY(), tb.getMaxZ());
        double reaction = pd.lastBigRot != 0 && now - pd.lastBigRot < 600 ? now - pd.lastBigRot : -1;

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- AutoClicker (hit rhythm / speed)
        Ring ivr = pd.ring("iv", 30);
        Ring times = pd.ring("hits", 40);
        if (prevAttack != 0 && now - prevAttack < 1500) ivr.add(Math.round((now - prevAttack) / 50.0));
        times.add(now);
        if (AC.enabled) {
            int cnt = 0;
            for (int k = 0; k < times.size(); k++) if (now - (long) times.get(k) <= 1000) cnt++;
            if (cnt > AC.p("max-cps")) {
                if (pd.bump("ac_cps", 1) >= 2) {
                    pd.setBuf("ac_cps", 0);
                    cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, AC, cnt + " hits in one second", 1.5);
                }
            } else {
                pd.bump("ac_cps", -0.5);
            }
            if (ivr.full()) {
                int[] counts = new int[32];
                int best = 0;
                for (int k = 0; k < ivr.size(); k++) {
                    int v = (int) Math.min(31, Math.max(0, ivr.get(k)));
                    if (++counts[v] > best) best = counts[v];
                }
                double ratio = best / (double) ivr.size();
                double meanTicks = ivr.mean();
                if (ratio >= AC.p("mode-ratio") && meanTicks > 0 && meanTicks <= 20.0 / AC.p("min-cps")) {
                    if (pd.bump("ac_mode", 1) >= 2) {
                        pd.setBuf("ac_mode", 0);
                        ivr.clear();
                        cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, AC, String.format("%.0f%% of clicks have the same delay", ratio * 100), 1.0);
                    }
                } else {
                    pd.bump("ac_mode", -0.25);
                }
            }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- mini AI sample
        pl.ai().onHit(pd, aimErr, now, reaction);

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- TriggerBot (constant, very short reaction)
        if (TRIG.enabled && reaction >= 0) {
            Ring rr = pd.ring("react", (int) Math.max(6, TRIG.p("samples")));
            rr.add(reaction);
            if (rr.full() && rr.mean() < TRIG.p("max-reaction-ms") && rr.std() < TRIG.p("max-std-ms")) {
                String info = String.format("camera-to-hit delay %.0fms +-%.0f over %d hits", rr.mean(), rr.std(), rr.size());
                rr.clear();
                cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, TRIG, info, 1.0);
            }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Criticals (fake airborne state)
        if (CRIT.enabled) {
            double fd = p.getFallDistance();
            Location l = p.getLocation();
            boolean candidate = !p.isOnGround() && fd > 0 && fd < CRIT.p("max-fall") && pd.airTicks <= 1
                    && !p.isInsideVehicle() && !p.isSwimming() && !pd.levitating && !pd.slowFalling
                    && l.getWorld() != null && Phys.env(l.getWorld(), l.getX(), l.getY(), l.getZ()) == 0
                    && Phys.support(l.getWorld(), l.getX(), l.getY(), l.getZ(), 0.25) != null
                    && Phys.support(l.getWorld(), l.getX(), l.getY(), l.getZ(), 0.25) != Phys.UNKNOWN;
            if (candidate) {
                if (pd.bump("crit", 1) >= CRIT.p("buffer")) {
                    pd.setBuf("crit", 0);
                    cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, CRIT, String.format("falling %.2f blocks while standing on the ground", fd), 1.0);
                }
            } else {
                pd.bump("crit", -0.5);
            }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- MultiAura / KillAura target switching
        boolean otherTarget = pd.lastTargetId != null && !pd.lastTargetId.equals(target.getUniqueId());
        if (otherTarget && MULTI.enabled && now - pd.lastTargetMs <= MULTI.p("same-tick-ms")) {
            cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, MULTI, "two different targets hit within " + (now - pd.lastTargetMs) + "ms", 1.0);
        }
        if (KA.enabled) {
            if (otherTarget && now - pd.lastTargetMs < KA.p("switch-ms")) {
                double dy = Math.abs(Geo.wrap(yaw - pd.lastHitYaw));
                if (dy > KA.p("switch-angle")) {
                    if (pd.bump("ka_sw", 1) >= KA.p("buffer")) {
                        pd.setBuf("ka_sw", 0);
                        cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, KA, String.format("snapped %.0f degrees to a new target in %dms", dy, now - pd.lastTargetMs), 1.0);
                    }
                } else {
                    pd.bump("ka_sw", -0.35);
                }
            } else {
                pd.bump("ka_sw", -0.1);
            }
            if (dist > KA.p("min-distance") && aimErr > KA.p("max-angle")) {
                cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, KA, String.format("hit a target %.0f degrees away from the crosshair", aimErr), 2.0);
            }
        }
        pd.lastTargetId = target.getUniqueId();
        pd.lastTargetMs = now;
        pd.lastHitYaw = yaw;

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- geometry (player vs player only)
        if (tp == null || pd2 == null || pd2.exotic || pd2.hCount < 3 || tp.isInsideVehicle() || tp.isGliding()
                || now - pd2.lastTeleport < 1500 || now - pd2.lastRespawn < 2000 || !tp.getWorld().equals(p.getWorld())) {
            return cancel;
        }
        long normal = Math.max(100, Math.min(s.maxRewindMs, pd.ping + s.rewindExtraMs));
        double limit = pd.reachEntity + REACH.p("tolerance");
        double minNorm = minDist(pd2, tp, ex, ey, ez, now, normal);
        if (minNorm > limit) {
            long ext = normal + (long) BACK.p("extra-ms");
            double minExt = minDist(pd2, tp, ex, ey, ez, now, ext);
            if (BACK.enabled && minExt <= limit) {
                cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, BACK, String.format("hit needs a position older than %dms (%.2f blocks away now)", normal, minNorm), 1.0);
            } else if (REACH.enabled) {
                cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, REACH, String.format("%.2f blocks (limit %.2f)", minNorm, limit), Math.min(4.0, 1.0 + (minNorm - limit) * 2));
            }
            return cancel;
        }

        // Hitbox: the crosshair (current or previous camera, current or previous eye) must be on the lag-compensated box
        if (HITBOX.enabled && dist > 0.5) {
            double maxT = limit + 1.5;
            double expand = HITBOX.p("expand");
            boolean hit = rayHit(ex, ey, ez, ldx, ldy, ldz, maxT, pd2, tp, now, normal, expand);
            MoveCtx prev = pd.prev;
            if (!hit && prev != null) {
                double pdx = Geo.dirX(prev.yaw, prev.pitch), pdy = Geo.dirY(prev.pitch), pdz = Geo.dirZ(prev.yaw, prev.pitch);
                double eh = p.getEyeHeight();
                hit = rayHit(ex, ey, ez, pdx, pdy, pdz, maxT, pd2, tp, now, normal, expand)
                        || rayHit(prev.x, prev.y + eh, prev.z, pdx, pdy, pdz, maxT, pd2, tp, now, normal, expand)
                        || rayHit(prev.x, prev.y + eh, prev.z, ldx, ldy, ldz, maxT, pd2, tp, now, normal, expand);
            }
            if (!hit) {
                cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, HITBOX, String.format("crosshair %.0f degrees off the target", aimErr), 1.0);
            }
        }

        // AimAssist rule: aim locked on the centre of a MOVING target again and again
        if (AIM.enabled && dist >= AIM.p("min-distance") && pd2.lastH > 0.12 && now - pd2.lastMoveMs < 250) {
            Ring ar = pd.ring("aim", (int) Math.max(6, AIM.p("window")));
            ar.add(aimErr);
            if (ar.full()) {
                int lock = ar.countBelow(AIM.p("lock-error"));
                if (lock >= AIM.p("min-lock")) {
                    ar.clear();
                    cancel |= pl.vio().flag(pd, AIM, lock + " of the last hits were within " + AIM.p("lock-error") + " degrees of the target centre", 1.0);
                }
            }
        }
        return cancel;
    }

    private boolean crystal(PlayerData pd, Entity target, long now) {
        if (!CRYSTAL.enabled) return false;
        long placed = pd.lastCrystalPlace;
        if (placed == 0 || now - placed > 2000) return false;
        Ring cx = pd.ring("cx", 1), cy = pd.ring("cy", 1), cz = pd.ring("cz", 1);
        if (cx.size() == 0) return false;
        Location tl = target.getLocation();
        double dx = tl.getX() - cx.get(0), dy = tl.getY() - cy.get(0), dz = tl.getZ() - cz.get(0);
        if (dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz > 36) return false; // not the crystal this player placed
        pd.lastCrystalPlace = 0; // one placement matches one detonation
        double react = now - placed - pd.ping;
        if (react < CRYSTAL.p("max-reaction-ms")) {
            if (pd.bump("crystal", 1) >= CRYSTAL.p("buffer")) {
                pd.setBuf("crystal", 0);
                return pl.vio().flag(pd, CRYSTAL, String.format("detonated %.0fms after placing (ping-adjusted)", Math.max(0, react)), 1.0);
            }
        } else {
            pd.bump("crystal", -1);
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ===================================================================================== bow

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onShoot(EntityShootBowEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getEntity() instanceof Player p) || !FASTBOW.enabled) return;
        ItemStack bow = e.getBow();
        if (bow == null || bow.getType() != Material.BOW) return; // crossbows are charged before shooting
        PlayerData pd = pl.data(p);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (pd.bypassed(now, pl.settings())) return;
        if (pd.useStartMs == 0 || !"BOW".equals(pd.useItem)) return;
        long elapsed = now - pd.useStartMs;
        if (elapsed > 8000 || elapsed < 0) return;
        double f = Math.max(0, (elapsed + FASTBOW.p("tolerance-ms")) / 50.0) / 20.0;
        double expected = Math.min(1.0, (f * f + f * 2.0) / 3.0);
        double force = e.getForce();
        if (force > 0.3 && force > expected + FASTBOW.p("force-slack")) {
            if (pl.vio().flag(pd, FASTBOW, String.format("force %.2f after %dms (max %.2f)", force, elapsed, expected), 1.0)) {
                e.setCancelled(true);
            }
        }
    }

    // ===================================================================================== potions / totems

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onLaunch(ProjectileLaunchEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getEntity() instanceof ThrownPotion tpot) || !(tpot.getShooter() instanceof Player p) || !AUTOPOT.enabled) return;
        PlayerData pd = pl.data(p);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (pd.bypassed(now, pl.settings())) return;
        if (pd.lastHeldMs != 0 && now - pd.lastHeldMs < AUTOPOT.p("min-delay-ms")) {
            if (pd.bump("autopot", 1) >= AUTOPOT.p("buffer")) {
                pd.setBuf("autopot", 0);
                pl.vio().flag(pd, AUTOPOT, "potion thrown " + (now - pd.lastHeldMs) + "ms after switching slot", 1.0);
            }
        } else {
            pd.bump("autopot", -0.5);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onResurrect(EntityResurrectEvent e) {
        if (e.getEntity() instanceof Player p) {
            PlayerData pd = pl.data(p);
            pd.lastTotemPop = System.currentTimeMillis();
            pd.ts("totemUsed", 0);
        }
    }

    private void totemEquip(Player p) {
        if (!TOTEM.enabled) return;
        PlayerData pd = pl.data(p);
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (pd.lastTotemPop == 0 || now - pd.lastTotemPop > 1500 || pd.ts("totemUsed") == pd.lastTotemPop) return;
        if (pd.bypassed(now, pl.settings())) return;
        pd.ts("totemUsed", pd.lastTotemPop);
        double react = now - pd.lastTotemPop - pd.ping;
        if (react < TOTEM.p("max-reaction-ms")) {
            if (pd.bump("totem", 1) >= TOTEM.p("buffer")) {
                pd.setBuf("totem", 0);
                pl.vio().flag(pd, TOTEM, String.format("totem re-equipped %.0fms after the pop (ping-adjusted)", Math.max(0, react)), 1.0);
            }
        } else {
            pd.bump("totem", -1);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onSwapHands(PlayerSwapHandItemsEvent e) {
        ItemStack off = e.getOffHandItem();
        if (off != null && off.getType() == Material.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING) totemEquip(e.getPlayer());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onTotemClick(InventoryClickEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player p)) return;
        ItemStack cur = e.getCurrentItem(), cursor = e.getCursor();
        boolean totem = (cur != null && cur.getType() == Material.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING)
                || (cursor != null && cursor.getType() == Material.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING);
        if (!totem && e.getHotbarButton() >= 0) {
            ItemStack hb = p.getInventory().getItem(e.getHotbarButton());
            totem = hb != null && hb.getType() == Material.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING;
        }
        if (totem) totemEquip(p);
    }
}

```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/Compat.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

import org.bukkit.attribute.Attribute;
import org.bukkit.attribute.AttributeInstance;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Small helpers that keep VyxAC working across 1.20.x and 1.21.x.
 * Attribute and potion constants were renamed between versions, so they are looked up by name at runtime
 * instead of being referenced directly.
 */
public final class Compat {
    private static final Map<String, Attribute> ATTR_CACHE = new HashMap<>();
    private static final Map<Integer, Double> APEX_CACHE = new HashMap<>();

    private static java.lang.reflect.Method PING;
    private static boolean pingInit;

    private Compat() {}

    /** Player ping without a compile-time dependency on Player#getPing (not present on every API version). */
    public static int ping(Player p) {
        try {
            if (!pingInit) {
                pingInit = true;
                try {
                    PING = Player.class.getMethod("getPing");
                } catch (Throwable t) {
                    PING = null;
                }
            }
            if (PING == null) return 0;
            return ((Number) PING.invoke(p)).intValue();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    public static Attribute attr(String... names) {
        String key = names[0];
        if (ATTR_CACHE.containsKey(key)) return ATTR_CACHE.get(key);
        Attribute found = null;
        for (String n : names) {
            try {
                Object o = Attribute.class.getField(n).get(null);
                if (o instanceof Attribute) {
                    found = (Attribute) o;
                    break;
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                // constant does not exist on this server version
            }
        }
        ATTR_CACHE.put(key, found);
        return found;
    }

    public static double attrValue(Player p, double def, String... names) {
        try {
            Attribute a = attr(names);
            if (a == null) return def;
            AttributeInstance ai = p.getAttribute(a);
            return ai == null ? def : ai.getValue();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return def;
        }
    }

    /** Effect level (amplifier + 1) of the first effect whose legacy or new name matches, 0 if none. */
    public static int level(Player p, String... names) {
        try {
            for (PotionEffect e : p.getActivePotionEffects()) {
                String n = e.getType().getName();
                if (n == null) continue;
                for (String want : names) {
                    if (n.equalsIgnoreCase(want)) return e.getAmplifier() + 1;
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            // fall through
        }
        return 0;
    }

    /** Highest point (blocks above the take-off point) a normal jump can reach, simulated with vanilla gravity. */
    public static double jumpApex(int jumpLvl, double jumpStrength) {
        double v = jumpStrength + 0.1 * jumpLvl;
        int key = (int) Math.round(v * 1000.0);
        Double cached = APEX_CACHE.get(key);
        if (cached != null) return cached;
        double y = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 400 && v > 0; i++) {
            y += v;
            v = (v - 0.08) * 0.98;
        }
        APEX_CACHE.put(key, y);
        return y;
    }
}
```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/CustomizeGui.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.ClickType;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryDragEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.Inventory;
import org.bukkit.inventory.InventoryHolder;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/** /vyxac customize: toggle checks and main features with clicks. */
public final class CustomizeGui implements Listener {
    private static final class Holder implements InventoryHolder {
        private Inventory inv;

        @Override
        public Inventory getInventory() {
            return inv;
        }
    }

    private static final String[] MODES = {"ALERT_ONLY", "REVIEW", "AUTO"};
    private final VyxAC pl;

    public CustomizeGui(VyxAC pl) {
        this.pl = pl;
    }

    public void open(Player p) {
        Holder h = new Holder();
        Inventory inv = Bukkit.createInventory(h, 54, Settings.color("&8VyxAC &7- customize"));
        h.inv = inv;
        fill(inv, p);
        p.openInventory(inv);
    }

    private ItemStack item(Material m, String name, List<String> lore) {
        ItemStack it = new ItemStack(m);
        ItemMeta meta = it.getItemMeta();
        if (meta != null) {
            meta.setDisplayName(Settings.color(name));
            List<String> l = new ArrayList<>();
            for (String s : lore) l.add(Settings.color(s));
            meta.setLore(l);
            it.setItemMeta(meta);
        }
        return it;
    }

    private void fill(Inventory inv, Player p) {
        inv.clear();
        int slot = 0;
        for (CheckInfo ci : pl.checks().all()) {
            if (slot > 47) break;
            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();
            lore.add("&8" + ci.category);
            lore.add("&7" + ci.description);
            lore.add("&7VL per flag: &f" + ci.vlPerFlag + "&7, punish VL: &f" + (int) ci.punishVl);
            lore.add("&7Counts as punishment evidence: " + (ci.punishable ? "&ayes" : "&cno"));
            lore.add("");
            lore.add("&eLeft-click: &7enable / disable");
            lore.add("&eRight-click: &7evidence on / off");
            inv.setItem(slot++, item(ci.enabled ? Material.LIME_DYE : Material.GRAY_DYE,
                    (ci.enabled ? "&a" : "&c") + ci.name, lore));
        }
        Settings s = pl.settings();
        inv.setItem(48, item(Material.COMPARATOR, "&bPunishment mode: &f" + s.punishMode,
                List.of("&7ALERT_ONLY: never punish", "&7REVIEW: staff decides with one click", "&7AUTO: punish when evidence is strong", "", "&eClick to cycle")));
        inv.setItem(49, item(Material.ENDER_EYE, "&bMini AI: " + (s.aiEnabled ? "&aon" : "&coff"), List.of("&eClick to toggle")));
        inv.setItem(50, item(Material.SHIELD, "&bMitigation: " + (s.mitigation ? "&aon" : "&coff"),
                List.of("&7Cancel flagged moves and hits", "", "&eClick to toggle")));
        inv.setItem(51, item(Material.CLOCK, "&bLag protection: " + (s.lagProtection ? "&aon" : "&coff"),
                List.of("&7Ignore flags while the server lags", "", "&eClick to toggle")));
        inv.setItem(52, item(Material.BELL, "&bMy alerts: " + (pl.vio().alertsEnabled(p.getUniqueId()) ? "&aon" : "&coff"), List.of("&eClick to toggle")));
        inv.setItem(53, item(Material.REDSTONE_TORCH, "&bReload config", List.of("&eClick to reload")));
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH)
    public void onClick(InventoryClickEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getInventory().getHolder() instanceof Holder)) return;
        e.setCancelled(true);
        if (!(e.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player p) || !p.hasPermission("vyxac.admin")) return;
        if (e.getClickedInventory() != e.getInventory()) return;
        int slot = e.getRawSlot();
        Settings s = pl.settings();
        if (slot >= 0 && slot <= 47) {
            int i = 0;
            for (CheckInfo ci : pl.checks().all()) {
                if (i++ != slot) continue;
                if (e.getClick() == ClickType.RIGHT) pl.setConfigValue("checks." + ci.name + ".punishable", !ci.punishable);
                else pl.setConfigValue("checks." + ci.name + ".enabled", !ci.enabled);
                break;
            }
        } else if (slot == 48) {
            int idx = 0;
            for (int k = 0; k < MODES.length; k++) if (MODES[k].equals(s.punishMode)) idx = k;
            pl.setConfigValue("punishments.mode", MODES[(idx + 1) % MODES.length]);
        } else if (slot == 49) {
            pl.setConfigValue("features.ai", !s.aiEnabled);
        } else if (slot == 50) {
            pl.setConfigValue("features.mitigation", !s.mitigation);
        } else if (slot == 51) {
            pl.setConfigValue("features.lag-protection", !s.lagProtection);
        } else if (slot == 52) {
            pl.vio().setAlerts(p.getUniqueId(), !pl.vio().alertsEnabled(p.getUniqueId()));
        } else if (slot == 53) {
            pl.loadAll();
        } else {
            return;
        }
        fill(e.getInventory(), p);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGH)
    public void onDrag(InventoryDragEvent e) {
        if (e.getInventory().getHolder() instanceof Holder) e.setCancelled(true);
    }
}
```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/Geo.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

/** Plain-double geometry helpers (no allocations in hot paths). Minecraft yaw/pitch convention. */
public final class Geo {
    private Geo() {}

    public static double dirX(float yaw, float pitch) {
        return -Math.sin(Math.toRadians(yaw)) * Math.cos(Math.toRadians(pitch));
    }

    public static double dirY(float pitch) {
        return -Math.sin(Math.toRadians(pitch));
    }

    public static double dirZ(float yaw, float pitch) {
        return Math.cos(Math.toRadians(yaw)) * Math.cos(Math.toRadians(pitch));
    }

    /** Wraps an angle difference into [-180, 180]. */
    public static float wrap(float a) {
        a %= 360f;
        if (a >= 180f) a -= 360f;
        if (a < -180f) a += 360f;
        return a;
    }

    /** Shortest distance from point (px,py,pz) to the axis aligned box. 0 when inside. */
    public static double pointBoxDist(double px, double py, double pz,
                                      double x0, double y0, double z0, double x1, double y1, double z1) {
        double dx = Math.max(Math.max(x0 - px, 0), px - x1);
        double dy = Math.max(Math.max(y0 - py, 0), py - y1);
        double dz = Math.max(Math.max(z0 - pz, 0), pz - z1);
        return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);
    }

    /** Slab test. True when the ray (origin o, direction d, length 0..maxT) hits the box. */
    public static boolean rayHitsBox(double ox, double oy, double oz, double dx, double dy, double dz, double maxT,
                                     double x0, double y0, double z0, double x1, double y1, double z1) {
        double tmin = 0, tmax = maxT;
        double[] o = {ox, oy, oz}, d = {dx, dy, dz}, lo = {x0, y0, z0}, hi = {x1, y1, z1};
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            if (Math.abs(d[i]) < 1e-9) {
                if (o[i] < lo[i] || o[i] > hi[i]) return false;
            } else {
                double t1 = (lo[i] - o[i]) / d[i], t2 = (hi[i] - o[i]) / d[i];
                if (t1 > t2) {
                    double t = t1;
                    t1 = t2;
                    t2 = t;
                }
                tmin = Math.max(tmin, t1);
                tmax = Math.min(tmax, t2);
                if (tmin > tmax) return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    /** Angle in degrees between direction (dx,dy,dz) and the vector (vx,vy,vz). */
    public static double angleDeg(double dx, double dy, double dz, double vx, double vy, double vz) {
        double dl = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz), vl = Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy + vz * vz);
        if (dl < 1e-9 || vl < 1e-9) return 0;
        double c = (dx * vx + dy * vy + dz * vz) / (dl * vl);
        return Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, c))));
    }
}
```

`VyxAC-source/VyxAC/src/main/java/com/vyxac/MoveCtx.java`:

```java
package com.vyxac;

/** Immutable snapshot of one movement event. */
public final class MoveCtx {
    public static final int WATER = 1, LAVA = 2, CLIMB = 4, WEB = 8, ICE = 16, BOUNCE = 32, HONEY = 64, SOUL = 128, BUBBLE = 256;
    /** environments in which normal gravity / ground rules do not apply */
    public static final int SPECIAL = WATER | LAVA | CLIMB | WEB | BOUNCE | HONEY | BUBBLE;

    public final long time;
    public final double x, y, z;
    public final float yaw, pitch;
    public final int env;
    public final boolean supported;

    public MoveCtx(long time, double x, double y, double z, float yaw, float pitch, int env, boolean supported) {
        this.time = time;
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.z = z;
        this.yaw = yaw;
        this.pitch = pitch;
        this.env = env;
        this.supported = supported;
    }
}
```
